Scalar f for golden: use vectorized for single x:
```
def coord_f_val(x):
    T = one_minus_y + x
    new = 2*x*a
    new[i] = x*x
    Qmax = np.max(rest + new)
    M = max(Qmax, max_unaff)
    return M/(T*T)
```
This allocates array each eval; okay.

Let's design a smooth gradient optimizer using log-softmax. This can be top-level and vectorized. Might be powerful.

Smooth gradient optimizer details:
```
def smooth_optimize(a, time_budget, rng, tau0=0.01, tau_min=0.0005):
   a = a / a.sum()
   u = np.log(a + 1e-300)  # but a positive
   lr=0.01 maybe, m,v for Adam
   tau start maybe 0.02*max_b? Since max_b ~0.006. τ = max_b*frac maybe 0.001. Or use relative to b: tau = max_b * frac. If τ too small, gradient dominated by single active (same subgrad) but log variables.
   for iterations:
      b=full_conv(a) # FFT or direct? n up to 2048; FFT for gradient; coordinate uses direct updates.
      M = max(b)
      tau = max(M*tau_frac, 1e-12)
      shift=b/tau; shift-=shift.max(); w=exp; w/=sum
      # G_i = sum_k w_k * p_{k-i}
      # Compute via convolution/correlation. Need G_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} w_{i+j} p_j? where i 0..n-1, j 0..n-1, k=i+j. This is cross-correlation of w and p. Let p length n. We can compute with FFT: G = ifft( FFT(w reversed?) * FFT(p) )? Let's derive.
      We want G_i = ∑_{j} w_{i+j} p_j. This is cross-correlation of w (length 2n-1) with p, not standard maybe. We can compute directly O(n^2) for n small? n=2048 O4M per iter, maybe okay limited? FFT:
      Define p padded length L≥2n-1. Define w padded. We need for i=0..n-1, dot p_j with w_{i+j}. This is (p ⋆ w)[i] where cross-correlation. In FFT: G = ifft( conj(FFT(p)) * FFT(w) )? Let's test: circular cross-correlation c_i = ∑_j p_j w_{(j-i) mod L}? Need align.
      Let sequences p_j length L, w_k length L. cross_cor = ifft(fft(p)*conj(fft(w))) gives ∑ p_j conj(w_{j-i}). We need ∑ p_j w_{j+i}. Let w_rev_m = w_{-m mod L}. Then ifft(fft(p)*conj(fft(w_rev)))? Let's simplify.
      We can compute using convolution: Let p indexed j. Define w_rev[t] = w_{L-1-t} maybe. Cross-correlation c_i = ∑_j p_j w_{j+i}. If define W shifted by i. Another way: G_i = ∑_{k} w_k p_{k-i} (where p out=0). This is convolution of w with reversed p? Let rev_p[m]=p[-m mod?] no wrap. For linear, to get for i=0..n-1, we can zero-pad and use FFT:
      G_i = ∑_{k} w_k p_{k-i}. This is cross-correlation. In numpy, `np.correlate(w, p, mode='valid')`? `np.correlate(w, p, mode='full')` gives c_m = ∑ w_{k} p_{k-m}? Let's check numpy correlate(a,v): c_k = ∑ a_n * conj(v_{n-k})? According docs: c_k = ∑_n a_{n+k} conj(v_n). If v=p length n, a=w length 2n-1, mode='valid' length 2n-1 - n +1 = n. c_k for k=0..n-1 = ∑_{n=0}^{n-1} w_{n+k} p_n. That's exactly G_k! Great. def coordinate_descent(a, b, time_budget, rng, max_sweeps=None, verbose=False):
```
```
    n = len(a)
    a = np.asarray(a,float); a = np.clip(a,0,None); a /= a.sum()
    b = full_conv_direct(a)? or FFT; then if best.
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score = 2*n*np.max(b)
    no_improve_sweeps=0
    sweep=0
    golden_iters = 24 if n<=512 else 18
    t0=time.monotonic()
    while time_budget > 0:
       order = np.random...? top-level rng.shuffle
       improved_sweep=False
       for i in order:
           if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
           y = a[i]
           # define precomputed arrays
           start=i
           end=i+n
           aff = b[start:end] # length n (since b len 2n-1, for i up to n-1, end <=2n-1)
           # max_unaff
           unaff = 0.0
           if start>0: unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(b[:start])))
           if end < b.size: unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(b[end:])))
           cur_contrib = 2*y*a.copy(); cur_contrib[i]=y*y
           rest = aff - cur_contrib
           # maybe ensure rest nonnegative small negative
           rest = np.maximum(rest, 0.0?) Actually due to numerical, tiny negative; but if rest negative significant? Should not. Clipping to 0 may bias. Use np.maximum(rest, 0.0) to avoid negative due to round? But rest can be negative? Since b includes contribution plus others nonnegative. Use small tolerance: rest[rest<0 & >-1e-12]=0.
           # current max_b = max(b) (score factor)
           cur_max = float(np.max(b))
           # candidate x range
           x0 = y
           hi = min(0.9, max(0.05, 4.0*y + 0.02))
           # evaluate at candidates and do bracketed golden
           best_x, best_f = coordinate_line_search(a, rest, unaff, i, y, x0, hi, golden_iters)
           if best_f < cur_max - 1e-15:
               T = 1-y+best_x
               scale=1/T
               T2=T*T
               # final Qmax
               new = 2*best_x*a
               new[i]=best_x*best_x
               Q = rest + new
               # update b
               b *= scale*scale? Wait b normalized old. We need b_new = old_b / T^2 for unaffected, Q/T^2 for affected. So b *= 1/T2, then b[start:end]=Q/T2.
               b *= (1.0/(T*T))
               b[start:end] = Q / (T*T)
               a *= scale
               a[i] = best_x * scale
               new_max = max(float(np.max(b)), maybe)
               if new_max < cur_max: improved_sweep=True
           # else maybe if best_f close and x different? don't update.
       sweep +=1
       if improved_sweep:
           no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve>=5:
              # random perturb
              a = a + rng.normal(0, 0.02/n? maybe element scale)
              a = np.clip(a,0,None); a/=a.sum(); b=full_conv(a) if no improvement? recompute.
              no_improve=0
       if score improved update best.
    return best
```

`coord_f_multi` for vector xs:
```
def coord_f_multi(xs, a, rest, unaff, y, i):
    T=1-y+xs
    new=2*xs[:,None]*a[None,:]
    if i < new.shape[1]: new[:,i]=xs*xs
    q=rest[None,:]+new
    qmax=np.max(q, axis=1)
    qmax=np.maximum(qmax, unaff)
    vals=qmax/(T*T)
    return vals
```

```
def full_conv(a):
   n=a.size; size=2*n-1; fsize=1<<(size-1).bit_length(); fa=rfft(a,fsize); return irfft(fa*fa,fsize)[:size]
```

Smooth Adam optimizer:
```
def smooth_adam(a, time_budget, rng, tau_frac=0.05, lr=0.05):
    n=a.size; a=a/a.sum(); b=full_conv(a); best...
    u=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); u-=u.mean()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    tau_frac_current=tau_frac; beta1=0.9; beta2=0.99
    t0=time.monotonic()
    while ...:
       # maybe adaptive lr? Use lr * sqrt((1-b2^t)/(1-b1^t))
       tau = max(1e-12, max(b)*tau_frac_current)
       shift = b/tau; shift-=shift.max(); w=exp; w/=sum
       G = null_corr(w,a) # length n
       g = G - a*np.sum(G)  # (factor dropped)
       # clip g
       m=b1*m+(1-b1)*g; v=b2*v+(1-b2)*g*g
       mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
       u -= lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       u -= u.mean()
       a=np.exp(u); a/=a.sum()
       b=full_conv(a)
       sc=2n*max(b)
       if sc<best...
       # decrease tau_frac slowly: tau_frac_current = max(0.002, tau_frac_current*0.999)
       t+=1
    return best
```
`null_corr`: compute G_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} w_{i+j} a_j. Use FFT:
```
def cross_corr_weight(w, a):
    n = len(a); L=2*n-1; fsize=...
    # w length L, a padded fsize
    fa = rfft(a, fsize)
    fw = rfft(w, fsize)
    # G = ifft(fw * conj(fa)) length fsize; first n
    G = irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), fsize)[:n]
    return G
```

Algorithm `adam_smooth` pseudo:
```
def adam_smooth(a, time_budget, rng, tau_start=0.2, tau_end=0.02, lr=0.05):
  a=normalize(a); b=full_conv(a); best...
  u=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); u-=u.mean(); m=v=zeros; t=0
  tau_scale=tau_start
  t0=...
  last_improve=0; noimp=0
  while time:
     M=max(b); tau=max(M*tau_scale, 1e-12)
     shift=b/tau; shift-=shift.max(); w=exp(shift); w/=w.sum()
     G=cross_corr(w,a)
     g=G - a*np.sum(G)
     g_scale = M if M>0 else 1
     g = g/g_scale
     # maybe clip to [-10,10]
     m=...; update u
     a=exp(u normalized)
     b=full_conv(a)
     sc=2n*max(b)
     if sc<best: best...
     # tau decay: tau_scale = max(tau_end, tau_start * (tau_end/tau_start)**(t/iterations_estimate) or *=0.995)
     # occasionally perturb if no improve
```
This uses gradient of smooth L with tau adaptive; but if τ changes, not exact gradient of L with τ(a), but okay heuristic.

Now generating α with recurrence:
```
def init_alpha(n):
   a=np.ones(n)
   for k in range(1,n): a[k]=a[k-1]*(2*k-1)/(2*k)
   return a/a.sum()
```
α0=1. This exact central binomial. For n=1024, a tail ~1/√(π i), sum ~? No overflow. Good.

`init_geom(n, r)`: a[i]=r**i. For r near1. If r=1 uniform. Generate r values.

`init_u(n)`: as before.
`init_noise(base, sigma)`: base*(1+sigma*rng.normal), clip, normalize.

Let's create a `local_polish` function that alternates coordinate sweeps with random pair transfers. Pair transfers can escape coordinate local minima that require moving mass between two coordinates simultaneously without changing sum. Coordinate with normalization moves mass relative to all; but pair transfer can move directly. We can occasionally do random pair line search. But O(n^2) if exhaustive; random O(total).
Random pair line search implementation: choose i,j. We can vary δ ∈ [min(0, -a_i)+?]. For pair transfer preserving sum (no normalization): a_i' = a_i + δ, a_j'=a_j - δ, δ∈[-a_i, a_j] if δ moves from j to i? Let's define moving δ from j to i: new_i = y_i + δ, new_j=y_j - δ, δ∈[0,y_j]. Objective after pair update (sum unchanged): b_k(δ) quadratic. Affected lags are union of ranges for i and j: [i,i+n-1] ∪ [j,j+n-1]. Could compute max over union O(2n). We can golden search δ. This move can be effective. Do e.g., 100 random pair moves per sweep if n small? But coordinate sweeps already many. We can after no improvement do 50 random pair moves.

Implement pair line search? Complexity moderate. Might not be necessary. But can add for perturb: simply choose two random coords and swap a fraction (not optimized) to escape. Coordinate will optimize after.

```
def random_transfer(a, rng, count, frac):
```

`adam_smooth` precise:
```
def adam_smooth(a, b, time_budget, rng, tau_start=0.3, tau_end=0.03, lr=0.05):
   n=a.size; a=clip_normalize(a); b=full_conv(a)
   best=a.copy(); best_b=b.copy(); best_sc=score
   u=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); u-=u.mean()
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   tau_scale=tau_start
   deadline=t0+time_budget
   noimp=0
   while time<deadline:
      M=float(np.max(b))
      tau=max(M*tau_scale, 1e-15)
      shift=b/tau; shift-=shift.max(); expw=np.exp(shift); sumw=expw.sum(); if sumw<=0: break; w=expw/sumw
      G=cross_corr(w,a)
      g=G - a*np.sum(G)
      scale = M if M>0 else 1.0
      g = g/scale
      # normalize g L2 maybe
      gnorm = np.linalg.norm(g)
      if gnorm>0: g /= gnorm * sqrt(n)? To keep step size? Use lr directly on normalized gradient. Maybe don't divide norm; g values ~? G maybe ~M? Divide by M gives O(1). L2 ~√n? If lr 0.05, update ~0.5/√n small. Use g = g / (np.linalg.norm(g)+1e-12) * np.sqrt(n) to make RMS 1. Then lr ~0.05.
```
Let's set `g = (G - a*sumG) / (M+eps)`. Its size maybe O(1). L2 ~√n? For n=128, rmse maybe1. lr=0.1 gives u step 0.1, okay.
Use:
```
gnorm = np.linalg.norm(g)
if gnorm > 1e-12:
    g = g / gnorm * np.sqrt(n) * 0.5 # target RMS 0.5
```
Maybe overkill. Simpler: `g = g / (M+1e-12)`. Adam handles scale. lr=0.05.
Update:
```
t +=1
m = 0.9*m + 0.1*g
v = 0.99*v + 0.01*g*g
mhat = m/(1-0.9**t); vhat = v/(1-0.99**t)
u = u - lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
u -= u.mean()
# To avoid too negative, keep within [-20,20]? clip.
u = np.clip(u, -30, 30)
a = np.exp(u); s=a.sum(); a/=s
b = full_conv(a)
sc=2n*max(b)
if sc<best_sc: best...; noimp=0 else noimp+=1
# tau anneal: tau_scale = tau_start * (tau_end/tau_start)**(t/est) maybe. Use *=0.999 until min.
if tau_scale>tau_end: tau_scale *= 0.998
if noimp>500:
   # random perturbation in u
   u += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n); ...
   noimp=0
```

```
def final_polish(a, time_budget, rng):
   a=clip_normalize(a); b=full_conv(a); best...
   total=time_budget
   t0=time.monotonic()
   final_floor=10
   while remaining > 30:
       # smooth portion 10-60s
       t_smooth = min(60, remaining*0.2)
       if t_smooth > 5: a,b=adam_smooth(a,b,t_smooth,rng,tau_start=0.2)
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       if rem < 20: break
       t_coord = rem - 5
       a,b=coordinate_descent(a,b,t_coord,rng, final=True)
       # If no improvement coordinate, do bigger random restart mix:
       if score not improved? maybe final_polish uses best from coordinate; coordinate has internal best.
   return best
```

```
def clip_normalize(a):
```

Let's detail `get_initializers`:
```
def get_initializers(n,rng,prev):
    init=[]
    base=init_alpha(n)
    init.append(base.copy())
    init.append(np.roll? reversed base? same but add without score maybe)
    init.append(clip_normalize(base[::-1].copy()))
    for gamma in [0.85,1.15]:
       init.append(clip_normalize(init_alpha_power_from_base(base,gamma)))
    for sigma in [0.25,0.6]:
       init.append(clip_normalize(np.maximum(base*(1+sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)),1e-12)))
    # shifted alpha
    sh=int(n*0.1)
    a=init_alpha_shift(n,sh)
    init.append(a)
    # geometric
    for r in [0.85,0.95]:
       a=np.power(r,np.arange(n,dtype=float)); init.append(clip_normalize(a))
    # random
    init.append(clip_normalize(np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))+0.1))
    # u shape
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float); m=np.minimum(i,n-1-i)+1; init.append(clip_normalize(1/np.sqrt(m)))
    if prev is not None:
       init.insert(0, interpolate_prev(prev,n))
    # maybe remove duplicates? no.
    return init
```
`init_alpha_shift`: compute base of length n+shift: `b=init_alpha(n+shift); return clip_normalize(b[shift:])`. If shift >=n? no.
`interpolate_prev`: if prev m, if n==1 return. `idx = np.linspace(0, m-1, n).round().astype(int); a=prev[idx]; return clip_normalize(a)`. Better linear interpolation? simple.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    start=time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget=float(budget_s)-3.0
        if total_budget<5: return fallback
        deadline=start+total_budget
        rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
        prev=get_previous()
        best_a=None; best_b=None; best_sc=np.inf
        # quick direct from prev and alpha various n? update best with direct evaluations (cheap)
        for n in [128,256]:
           a=init_alpha(n); sc=eval_internal(a); update
        # define job_specs
        specs=[]
        n_jobs_counts={64:3,128:8,256:5,512:4,1024:3,2048:2}
        n_time={64:10,128:22,256:22,512:28,1024:34,2048:45}
        for n in [128,256,512,1024]: maybe if total_budget large include 2048
           inits=get_initializers(n,rng,prev)
           cnt=n_jobs_counts.get(n,3)
           for idx in range(min(cnt,len(inits))): specs.append((n,inits[idx],n_time.get(n,20)))
        # Shuffle? Keep alpha first? We want diverse. Maybe sort by n? Use as generated; alpha first for each n.
        for n,a,tb in specs:
           if deadline-time.monotonic() < 5: break
           tb=min(tb, deadline-time.monotonic()-2)
           if tb<4: continue
           try:
              b=full_conv(a)
              if tb>12:
                 t0=time.monotonic(); smooth_time=min(0.35*tb,15)
                 a,b=adam_smooth(a,b,smooth_time,rng,tau_start=0.5)
                 tb-=time.monotonic()-t0
              if tb>3:
                 a,b=coordinate_descent(a,b,tb,rng)
              sc=score_from_b(b,n)
              if sc<best_sc: update
           except Exception: pass
           print progress
        if best_a is None: best_a=init_alpha(128); best_b=full_conv; best_sc=...
        # final phase: maybe if best n too small and time, run a couple final candidates from different n? no.
        final_time=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if final_time>20:
            # We can also do a separate final polish for best and maybe for previous if significantly different? only best.
            try:
               best_a,best_b=final_polish(best_a, best_b, final_time, rng)
               best_sc=score_from_b(best_b,best_a.size)
            except: ...
        # final convert and evaluate
        result=[float(x) for x in best_a]
        # ensure sum>0.01 and finite
        if any not finite or sum <0.01: fallback
        # optional verify
        try: ev=evaluate_sequence(result); print
        except: ev=np.inf
        if not finite or ev>best_sc*1.2+1e-9: fallback
        return result
    except Exception:
        return [float(x) for x in init_alpha(128)]
```

`final_polish`:
```
def final_polish(a, b, time_budget, rng):
    a=clip_normalize(a); b=full_conv(a); best...
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+time_budget
    last_score=best_sc
    while deadline-time.monotonic() > 30:
        chunk = min(240, deadline-time.monotonic()-20)
        if chunk<20: chunk=deadline-time.monotonic()-10
        if chunk<=0: break
        # smooth small
        t_smooth = min(30, chunk*0.15)
        if t_smooth>3:
            a,b=adam_smooth(a,b,t_smooth,rng,tau_start=0.25)
        rem = deadline-time.monotonic()-5
        if rem>10:
            a,b=coordinate_descent(a,b,rem,rng,golden_iters=16 if n<=512 else 10, verbose=False)
        sc=score_from_b(b,n)
        if sc >= last_score - 1e-7:
            # if stall, mix with fresh alpha
            base=init_alpha(n)
            a=clip_normalize(0.75*best_a + 0.25*base)
            a=clip_normalize(a + 0.05*(np.linalg.norm(a))*rng.standard_normal(n))
            b=full_conv(a)
        last_score = min(last_score, sc)
    return best_a,best_b
```

Detailed `coordinate_descent`:
```
def coordinate_descent(a, b, time_budget, rng, golden_iters=16, verbose=False):
    n=a.size
    a=clip_normalize(a)
    b=full_conv(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_b=b.copy(); best_sc=score_from_b(b,n)
    if time_budget<=0: return best_a,best_b
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,time_budget)
    sweeps=0; no_imp=0
    if golden_iters is None: golden_iters=16 if n<=512 else 10
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if sweeps % 8 ==0:
          # recompute to clean
          a=clip_normalize(a); b=full_conv(a); sc=score_from_b(b,n); if sc<best_sc: ...
       order=np.arange(n)
       rng.shuffle(order)
       improved_sweep=False
       for ii in range(n):
          if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
          i=int(order[ii])
          y=float(a[i])
          if not np.isfinite(y) or y<0: y=0
          start=i; end=i+n
          aff=b[start:end]
          unaff=0.0
          if start>0:
             unaff=max(unaff, float(np.max(b[:start])))
          if end < b.size:
             unaff=max(unaff, float(np.max(b[end:])))
          cur=2.0*y*a
          cur[i]=y*y
          rest=aff - cur
          rest=np.maximum(rest,0.0)
          x_hi = min(0.95, max(0.15, 3.0*y + 0.05))
          if y > x_hi: x_hi = min(0.99, y*1.05)
          best_x,best_f = coordinate_line_search(a, rest, unaff, i, y, x_hi, golden_iters)
          if best_f < np.max(b) - 1e-14:
              T=1.0 - y + best_x
              if T>1e-15:
                 invT=1.0/T; invT2=invT*invT
                 new=2.0*best_x*a
                 new[i]=best_x*best_x
                 Q=rest+new
                 b *= invT2
                 b[start:end] = Q * invT2
                 a *= invT
                 a[i] = best_x*invT
                 new_sc=score_from_b(b,n)
                 if new_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                    best_sc=new_sc; best_a=a.copy(); best_b=b.copy(); improved_sweep=True
              else: improved_sweep=False
       sweeps +=1
       if not improved_sweep:
          no_imp +=1
          if no_imp >= 3:
             a=random_transfer(best_a.copy(), rng, count=min(50,n), frac=0.25)
             b=full_conv(a)
             sc=score_from_b(b,n)
             if sc < best_sc: ...
             no_imp=0
       if verbose and sweeps%10==0: print...
    return best_a,best_b
```

`get_previous`:
```
def get_previous():
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
        h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, None)
    if h.ndim != 1 or h.size < 2 or h.sum() <= 0.01:
        return None
    return h / h.sum()
```
If h has length huge >5000? We may use interpolation. For n list, prev len maybe 128.

`interpolate_prev`:
```
def interpolate_prev(prev, n):
    m=prev.size
    if n==1: return np.array([float(prev.sum() or 1)])
    if m==1: return np.full(n, prev[0]/n)
    idx = np.linspace(0, m-1, n)
    left = np.floor(idx).astype(int); right=np.ceil(idx).astype(int); right=np.minimum(right,m-1)
    frac=(idx-left).astype(float)
    a=prev[left]*(1-frac)+prev[right]*frac
    return clip_normalize(a)
```
Good.

`init_alpha_shift`:
```
def init_alpha_shift(n, shift):
    if shift<=0: return init_alpha(n)
    base=init_alpha(n+shift)
    return clip_normalize(base[shift:])
```

`init_base_power(base,gamma)`: `a = np.power(np.maximum(base,1e-300), gamma); return clip_normalize(a)`.

Now `coordinate_line_search` implementation with grid:
```
def coordinate_line_search(a, rest, unaff, i, y, x_hi, golden_iters):
    x_min=1e-10
    x_hi=min(0.9, max(0.2, x_hi, y*1.05))
    if not np.isfinite(x_hi) or x_hi <= x_min: x_hi=0.2
    # build grid
    if x_hi > x_min:
       lin = np.linspace(x_min, x_hi, 10)
       # logspace can fail x_min 0; use x_min*10? Actually geomspace from x_min to x_hi with 10
       try: geo = np.geomspace(x_min, x_hi, 10)
       except: geo = lin
       extras = np.array([y, y*0.5, y*1.5, x_min*10, x_hi*0.8], dtype=float)
       xs = np.concatenate([lin,geo,extras])
    else: xs=np.array([x_min])
    xs=xs[np.isfinite(xs) & (xs>=x_min) & (xs<=x_hi)]
    if len(xs)==0: xs=np.array([x_min])
    xp=np.unique(xs)
    for _ in range(2):
       vals=coord_f_multi(xp,a,rest,unaff,y,i)
       idx=int(np.argmin(vals))
       best_x=float(xp[idx]); best_val=float(vals[idx])
       if idx == len(xp)-1 and len(xp)>1 and best_val <= vals[-2]* (1.0 - 1e-6) and x_hi < 0.9:
           x_hi=min(0.9, x_hi*1.5)
           # rebuild xs adding new hi
           newpts = np.array([x_hi*0.8, x_hi], dtype=float)
           xp = np.unique(np.concatenate([xp, newpts]))
       else: break
    width = max(0.01, 0.15*(x_hi-x_min), 0.5*abs(best_x - x_min))
    lo = max(x_min, best_x - width)
    hi = min(x_hi, best_x + width)
    if hi - lo <= 1e-12:
        return best_x,best_val
    gr=(math.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    c = hi - gr*(hi-lo)
    d = lo + gr*(hi-lo)
    fc = coord_f_scalar(c,a,rest,unaff,y,i)
    fd = coord_f_scalar(d,a,rest,unaff,y,i)
    for _ in range(golden_iters):
       if fc < fd:
          hi=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=hi-gr*(hi-lo); fc=coord_f_scalar(c,...)
       else:
          lo=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=lo+gr*(hi-lo); fd=coord_f_scalar(d,...)
    mid=0.5*(lo+hi); fm=coord_f_scalar(mid,...)
    if fm < best_val: best_val=fm; best_x=mid
    if fc < best_val: ...
    return best_x,best_val
```
Need if `coord_f_scalar` returns inf? It should finite.

`coord_f_multi` with geomspace: If x_min=1e-10, geomspace works. Good.

`coord_f_scalar`: use local `if x < x_min: return 1e300`. Also if T <= 0.

Use `float(np.max(...))` to avoid array.

Potential optimization: In `coordinate_line_search`, `a` old not copy. Good.

`coord_f_multi`: If `xs` large, `new = 2*xs[:,None]*a[None,:]`; if `xs` length 1, okay. `new[:,i]=xs*xs`. `q = rest[None, :] + new`. `qmax = np.max(q, axis=1)`. If `unaff` bigger, `np.maximum`. `vals = qmax/(T*T)`. Ensure T positive. If any x < x_min? filtered. Return vals.

Potential memory when n=2048, xs 25, new 25*2048=51k floats, okay.

Now `adam_smooth` details with time. We need `full_conv` to return b. Use `b` input? We can recompute at start.
```
def adam_smooth(a,b,time_budget,rng,tau_start=0.5,tau_end=0.02,lr=0.05):
   a=clip_normalize(a); b=full_conv(a); best...
   if time_budget<=0: return
   u=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); u-=u.mean()
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   tau_scale=tau_start
   no_imp=0; deadline=...
   while time<deadline:
      M_score=score; M=max(b)
      if M<=0: break
      tau=max(M*tau_scale, 1e-15)
      shift=b/tau; shift-=shift.max(); expw=np.exp(shift); sw=expw.sum()
      if sw<=0: w=np.zeros; w[max]=1 else w=expw/sw
      G=cross_corr(w,a)
      g=G - a*float(np.sum(G))
      g = g / (M + 1e-15)
      # normalize rms
      rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g)) + 1e-15
      g = g / rms * 0.5
      t +=1
      beta1=0.9; beta2=0.99
      m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*g
      v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*g*g
      mhat = m/(1-beta1**t)
      vhat = v/(1-beta2**t)
      u = u - lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
      u -= u.mean()
      u = np.clip(u, -30, 30)
      a = np.exp(u); s=a.sum(); a /= s
      b = full_conv(a)
      sc=score_from_b(b,n)
      if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best... no_imp=0
      else:
          no_imp +=1
      if tau_scale > tau_end:
          tau_scale *= 0.998
      if no_imp > 400:
          u += 0.03*rng.standard_normal(n); u-=u.mean(); ...
          tau_scale = max(tau_scale, 0.2)
          no_imp=0
    return best_a,best_b
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
using:
  - good algebraic initializers (central-binomial / flat-square-root seed,
    powers, shifts, random perturbations, previous best),
  - a log-smoothed gradient optimizer in softmax coordinates,
  - exact cyclic coordinate descent with 1-D line searches,
  - random-transfer restarts when coordinate descent stalls.
The final answer is normalized to sum 1, so the evaluator's clipping is inactive.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np


def clip_normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def full_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 1024:
        b = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        fsize = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fsize)
        b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, fsize)[:size]
    b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def score_from_b(b, n):
    mb = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mb


def cross_corr(w, a):
    """
    Return G[i] = sum_j w[i+j] * a[j], for i=0..n-1, where a has length n
    and w has length 2n-1.
    """
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    fsize = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fsize)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, fsize)
    g = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), fsize)[:n]
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(g, 0.0)


def adam_smooth(a, b, time_budget, rng, tau_start=0.5, tau_end=0.02, lr=0.08):
    a = clip_normalize(a)
    b = full_conv(a)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_b(b, n)

    if time_budget is None or float(time_budget) <= 0:
        return best_a, best_b

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    u = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    u -= float(np.mean(u))
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    no_imp = 0
    tau_scale = float(tau_start)

    beta1 = 0.90
    beta2 = 0.99

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        M = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
            break

        tau = max(M * tau_scale, 1e-15)
        shift = b / tau
        shift -= float(np.max(shift))
        ew = np.exp(shift)
        sw = float(np.sum(ew))
        if sw <= 1e-300:
            idx = int(np.argmax(b))
            w = np.zeros(b.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = 1.0
        else:
            w = ew / sw

        g_base = cross_corr(w, a)
        g = g_base - a * float(np.sum(g_base))
        g = g / (M + 1e-15)

        rms = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(g * g)) + 1e-20)
        g = (g / rms) * 0.5

        t += 1
        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * g
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (g * g)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - beta1 ** t)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - beta2 ** t)

        u = u - lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
        u -= float(np.mean(u))
        u = np.clip(u, -30.0, 30.0)

        a = np.exp(u)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            a = clip_normalize(best_a)
        else:
            a /= s

        b = full_conv(a)
        sc = score_from_b(b, n)

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_b = b.copy()
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1

        if tau_scale > tau_end:
            tau_scale *= 0.998

        if no_imp > 500:
            u += 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            u -= float(np.mean(u))
            u = np.clip(u, -30.0, 30.0)
            a = np.exp(u)
            s = float(np.sum(a))
            if s > 0:
                a /= s
            b = full_conv(a)
            sc = score_from_b(b, n)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_b = b.copy()
            tau_scale = max(tau_scale, 0.20)
            no_imp = 0

    return best_a, best_b


def coord_f_multi(xs, a, rest, unaff, y, i):
    xs = np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.float64)
    if xs.size == 0:
        return np.array([1e300], dtype=np.float64)
    T = 1.0 - y + xs
    good = np.isfinite(T) & (T > 1e-15) & (xs > 0.0)
    xs = xs[good]
    if xs.size == 0:
        return np.array([1e300], dtype=np.float64)
    T = T[good]

    new = 2.0 * xs[:, None] * a[None, :]
    new[:, i] = xs * xs
    q = rest[None, :] + new
    qmax = np.max(q, axis=1)
    qmax = np.maximum(qmax, unaff)
    return qmax / (T * T)


def coord_f_scalar(x, a, rest, unaff, y, i):
    if not np.isfinite(x) or x <= 0.0:
        return 1e300
    T = 1.0 - y + x
    if not np.isfinite(T) or T <= 1e-15:
        return 1e300
    new = 2.0 * x * a
    new[i] = x * x
    qmax = float(np.max(rest + new))
    if qmax < unaff:
        qmax = float(unaff)
    qmax = max(0.0, qmax)
    return qmax / (T * T)


def coordinate_line_search(a, rest, unaff, i, y, x_hi, golden_iters):
    x_min = 1e-10
    if not np.isfinite(x_hi):
        x_hi = 0.2
    x_hi = min(0.999, max(0.20, float(x_hi), y * 1.05 + 1e-12))
    if x_hi <= x_min:
        x_hi = 0.20

    xs_parts = []
    try:
        xs_parts.append(np.geomspace(x_min, x_hi, 12))
    except Exception:
        pass
    xs_parts.append(np.linspace(x_min, x_hi, 12))
    extras = [
        x_min * 10.0,
        y * 0.25,
        y * 0.5,
        y * 0.75,
        y,
        y * 1.1,
        y * 1.5,
        x_hi * 0.8,
        x_hi * 0.9,
        x_hi,
    ]
    xs_parts.append(np.asarray(extras, dtype=np.float64))
    xs = np.concatenate(xs_parts)
    xs = xs[np.isfinite(xs) & (xs >= x_min) & (xs <= x_hi)]
    if xs.size == 0:
        xs = np.array([x_min], dtype=np.float64)
    xp = np.unique(xs)

    best_x = float(x_min)
    best_val = 1e300

    for _ in range(2):
        vals = coord_f_multi(xp, a, rest, unaff, y, i)
        if vals.size == 0:
            break
        idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
        best_x = float(xp[idx])
        best_val = float(vals[idx])
        if idx == xs.size - 1 and len(xp) > 1 and best_val <= vals[-2] * (1.0 - 1e-6) and x_hi < 0.999:
            x_hi = min(0.999, x_hi * 1.6)
            xp = np.unique(np.concatenate((xp, np.array([x_hi * 0.8, x_hi], dtype=np.float64))))
        else:
            break

    width = max(0.01, 0.18 * max(0.0, x_hi - x_min), 0.6 * abs(best_x - x_min))
    lo = max(x_min, best_x - width)
    hi = min(x_hi, best_x + width)
    if hi - lo <= 1e-13:
        return best_x, best_val

    gr = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    c = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
    d = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
    fc = coord_f_scalar(c, a, rest, unaff, y, i)
    fd = coord_f_scalar(d, a, rest, unaff, y, i)

    for _ in range(int(golden_iters)):
        if fc < fd:
            hi = d
            d = c
            fd = fc
            c = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
            fc = coord_f_scalar(c, a, rest, unaff, y, i)
        else:
            lo = c
            c = d
            fc = fd
            d = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
            fd = coord_f_scalar(d, a, rest, unaff, y, i)

    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    fm = coord_f_scalar(mid, a, rest, unaff, y, i)

    if fm < best_val:
        best_val, best_x = fm, mid
    if fc < best_val:
        best_val, best_x = fc, c
    if fd < best_val:
        best_val, best_x = fd, d

    return best_x, best_val


def random_transfer(a, rng, count=20, frac=0.25):
    a = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    n = a.size
    for _ in range(int(count)):
        i = int(rng.integers(n))
        j = int(rng.integers(n))
        if i == j:
            continue
        if a[i] >= a[j]:
            src, tgt = i, j
        else:
            src, tgt = j, i
        amt = frac * float(a[src])
        if amt <= 0.0:
            continue
        a[src] -= amt
        a[tgt] += amt
    return clip_normalize(a)


def coordinate_descent(a, b, time_budget, rng, golden_iters=16, recompute_every=8):
    n = a.size
    a = clip_normalize(a)
    b = full_conv(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_b(b, n)

    if time_budget is None or float(time_budget) <= 0:
        return best_a, best_b

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    sweeps = 0
    no_imp = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if sweeps % max(1, int(recompute_every)) == 0:
            a = clip_normalize(a)
            b = full_conv(a)
            sc = score_from_b(b, n)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_b = b.copy()

        order = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        rng.shuffle(order)
        sweep_improved = False

        for pos in range(n):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break

            i = int(order[pos])
            y = float(a[i])
            if not np.isfinite(y) or y < 0.0:
                y = 0.0
                a[i] = 0.0

            start = i
            end = i + n

            unaff = 0.0
            if start > 0:
                unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(b[:start])))
            if end < b.size:
                unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(b[end:])))

            cur = 2.0 * y * a
            cur[i] = y * y
            rest = b[start:end] - cur
            rest = np.maximum(rest, 0.0)

            x_hi = min(0.999, max(0.20, 3.0 * y + 0.05, y * 1.05))
            best_x, best_f = coordinate_line_search(a, rest, unaff, i, y, x_hi, golden_iters)

            cur_max = float(np.max(b))
            if best_f < cur_max - 1e-14:
                T = 1.0 - y + best_x
                if T > 1e-15:
                    invT = 1.0 / T
                    invT2 = invT * invT
                    new = 2.0 * best_x * a
                    new[i] = best_x * best_x
                    q = rest + new

                    b *= invT2
                    b[start:end] = q * invT2

                    a *= invT
                    a[i] = best_x * invT

                    new_sc = score_from_b(b, n)
                    if new_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                        best_sc = new_sc
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        best_b = b.copy()
                        sweep_improved = True

        sweeps += 1

        if not sweep_improved:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 3:
                a = random_transfer(best_a, rng, count=max(20, min(80, n)), frac=0.35)
                b = full_conv(a)
                sc = score_from_b(b, n)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_b = b.copy()
                no_imp = 0

    return best_a, best_b


def init_alpha(n):
    a = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        return a / a.sum()
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, int(n)):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)
    return a / a.sum()


def init_alpha_power(n, gamma):
    base = init_alpha(n)
    base = np.maximum(base, 1e-300)
    a = np.power(base, float(gamma))
    return clip_normalize(a)


def init_alpha_shift(n, shift):
    shift = max(0, int(shift))
    if shift == 0:
        return init_alpha(n)
    base = init_alpha(n + shift)
    return clip_normalize(base[shift:])


def init_geometric(n, r):
    r = float(min(0.999, max(0.5, r)))
    i = np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(r, i)
    return clip_normalize(a)


def init_random_positive(n, rng):
    a = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(int(n))) + 0.05
    return a / a.sum()


def init_noisy_uniform(n, rng):
    a = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) + 0.8 * rng.standard_normal(int(n))
    a = np.abs(a) + 0.01
    return a / a.sum()


def init_u_shape(n):
    i = np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    m = np.minimum(i, float(n) - 1.0 - i) + 1.0
    a = 1.0 / np.sqrt(m)
    return a / a.sum()


def interpolate_prev(prev, n):
    m = prev.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(int(n), float(prev[0]) / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    idx = np.linspace(0.0, float(m - 1), int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    left = np.floor(idx).astype(np.int64)
    right = np.ceil(idx).astype(np.int64)
    right = np.minimum(right, m - 1)
    left = np.minimum(left, m - 1)
    frac = idx - left.astype(np.float64)
    a = prev[left] * (1.0 - frac) + prev[right] * frac
    return clip_normalize(a)


def get_previous():
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
        h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, None)
    if h.ndim != 1 or h.size < 2 or float(np.sum(h)) <= 0.01:
        return None
    return h / float(np.sum(h))


def get_initializers(n, rng, prev):
    inits = []
    n = int(n)

    alpha = init_alpha(n)
    inits.append(alpha.copy())
    inits.append(clip_normalize(alpha[::-1].copy()))

    inits.append(init_alpha_power(n, 0.85))
    inits.append(init_alpha_power(n, 1.15))
    inits.append(init_alpha_shift(n, max(1, n // 12)))

    for sig in (0.20, 0.55):
        a = alpha * (1.0 + sig * rng.standard_normal(n))
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
        inits.append(clip_normalize(a))

    inits.append(init_geometric(n, 0.88))
    inits.append(init_geometric(n, 0.96))
    inits.append(init_random_positive(n, rng))
    inits.append(init_noisy_uniform(n, rng))
    inits.append(init_u_shape(n))

    if prev is not None:
        inits.insert(0, interpolate_prev(prev, n))

    return inits


def add_top(top, sc, a, max_keep=4):
    if a is None or a.size == 0:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    top.append((float(sc), np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)))
    if len(top) > int(max_keep):
        max_i = 0
        for k in range(1, len(top)):
            if top[k][0] > top[max_i][0]:
                max_i = k
        del top[max_i]


def final_polish(a, b, time_budget, rng):
    a = clip_normalize(a)
    b = full_conv(a)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_b(b, n)

    if time_budget is None or float(time_budget) <= 0:
        return best_a, best_b

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    last_best = best_sc

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        chunk = min(300.0, max(20.0, remaining - 15.0))

        t_smooth = min(35.0, 0.18 * chunk)
        if t_smooth > 3.0:
            a, b = adam_smooth(a, b, t_smooth, rng, tau_start=0.35, tau_end=0.03, lr=0.07)
            sc = score_from_b(b, n)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_b = b.copy()

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 8.0
        if rem <= 5.0:
            break

        a, b = coordinate_descent(a, b, rem, rng,
                                  golden_iters=16 if n <= 512 else 10,
                                  recompute_every=6)
        sc = score_from_b(b, n)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_b = b.copy()

        if best_sc >= last_best - 1e-8:
            base = init_alpha(n)
            a = 0.72 * best_a + 0.28 * base
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            a = clip_normalize(a)
            a = clip_normalize(a + 0.05 * math.sqrt(float(n)) * rng.standard_normal(n))
            b = full_conv(a)
            sc = score_from_b(b, n)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_b = b.copy()
        else:
            last_best = best_sc

    return best_a, best_b


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = max(5.0, float(budget_s) - 4.0)
        deadline = start + total
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

        prev = get_previous()

        top = []

        # Cheap direct seeds.
        for n in (64, 128, 256):
            try:
                a = init_alpha(n)
                b = full_conv(a)
                sc = score_from_b(b, n)
                add_top(top, sc, a)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if prev is not None:
            try:
                n_prev = int(prev.size)
                if 32 <= n_prev <= 4096:
                    b = full_conv(prev)
                    sc = score_from_b(b, n_prev)
                    add_top(top, sc, prev)
                    # also a resampled version to common length
                    a128 = interpolate_prev(prev, 128)
                    b128 = full_conv(a128)
                    add_top(top, score_from_b(b128, 128), a128)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Search jobs: (n, initializer, base_time, optional_smooth)
        job_counts = {
            64: 2,
            128: 8,
            256: 5,
            512: 4,
            1024: 3,
            2048: 1,
        }
        job_times = {
            64: 10.0,
            128: 20.0,
            256: 22.0,
            512: 28.0,
            1024: 35.0,
            2048: 45.0,
        }

        if total > 1800.0:
            n_list = [128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048]
        else:
            n_list = [128, 256, 512, 1024]

        jobs = []
        for n in n_list:
            try:
                inits = get_initializers(n, rng, prev)
                cnt = int(job_counts.get(n, 3))
                tt = float(job_times.get(n, 20.0))
                for ii in range(min(cnt, len(inits))):
                    jobs.append((int(n), inits[ii], tt, True))
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Run exploratory jobs.
        for job in jobs:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
                break
            n, init_a, tb, use_smooth = job
            tb = min(tb, max(3.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0))
            if tb <= 2.0:
                continue
            try:
                a = clip_normalize(init_a)
                b = full_conv(a)
                sc = score_from_b(b, n)
                add_top(top, sc, a)

                if use_smooth and tb > 12.0:
                    smooth_time = min(0.38 * tb, 18.0)
                    t0 = time.monotonic()
                    a, b = adam_smooth(a, b, smooth_time, rng, tau_start=0.55, tau_end=0.04, lr=0.08)
                    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
                    sc = score_from_b(b, n)
                    add_top(top, sc, a)
                    tb -= elapsed

                if tb > 4.0:
                    gi = 14 if n <= 256 else (11 if n <= 1024 else 8)
                    a, b = coordinate_descent(a, b, tb, rng, golden_iters=gi, recompute_every=7)
                    sc = score_from_b(b, n)
                    add_top(top, sc, a)

                if len(top) == 1:
                    print(f"[search] n={n} sc={sc:.7f}")
            except Exception:
                pass

        if len(top) == 0:
            a = init_alpha(128)
            b = full_conv(a)
            sc = score_from_b(b, 128)
            add_top(top, sc, a)

        # Pick best.
        bi = 0
        best_sc = top[0][0]
        for k in range(1, len(top)):
            if top[k][0] < best_sc:
                best_sc = top[k][0]
                bi = k
        best_a = top[bi][1].copy()

        # Final polishing loop.
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 25.0:
            try:
                best_a, best_b = final_polish(best_a, None, max(1.0, remaining - 2.0), rng)
                best_sc = score_from_b(best_b, best_a.size)
                add_top(top, best_sc, best_a)
                print(f"[final] n={best_a.size} sc={best_sc:.7f}")
            except Exception:
                pass

        # If much time remains and a distinct runner-up exists, give it a short
        # chance too; this helps avoid overcommitting to a local basin.
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if len(top) > 1 and remaining > 70.0:
            # Find the best candidate different from current best by length or content.
            alt_i = -1
            alt_sc = float("inf")
            for k, (sc, seq) in enumerate(top):
                if seq.size == best_a.size:
                    try:
                        same = bool(np.allclose(seq, best_a, rtol=1e-4, atol=1e-6))
                    except Exception:
                        same = False
                    if same and abs(sc - best_sc) <= 1e-8:
                        continue
                if sc < alt_sc:
                    alt_sc = sc
                    alt_i = k
            if alt_i >= 0:
                alt_a, alt_b = top[alt_i]
                alt_a, alt_b = final_polish(alt_a.copy(), None, min(120.0, max(30.0, remaining - 20.0)), rng)
                sc = score_from_b(alt_b, alt_a.size)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_a = alt_a.copy()
                    best_b = alt_b.copy()
                    best_sc = sc
                    print(f"[alt-final] n={best_a.size} sc={best_sc:.7f}")

        # Fallback guarantee.
        if best_a is None or best_a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)):
            best_a = init_alpha(128)

        result = [float(x) for x in best_a]

        # Safety: keep the evaluator from clipping. Values are small, but enforce.
        s = sum(result)
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.01:
            best_a = init_alpha(128)
            result = [float(x) for x in best_a]

        if any(x < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(x) or x > 1000.0 for x in result):
            best_a = clip_normalize(best_a)
            result = [float(x) for x in best_a]

        try:
            ev = evaluate_sequence(result)
            print(f"[evaluator] n={len(result)} score={ev:.7f}; internal={best_sc:.7f}")
        except Exception:
            ev = float("inf")

        if not np.isfinite(ev) or ev > max(best_sc, 1.0) + 1e-7:
            fb = init_alpha(128)
            result = [float(x) for x in fb]
            try:
                ev2 = evaluate_sequence(result)
                print(f"[fallback] n={len(result)} score={ev2:.7f}")
            except Exception:
                pass

        return result

    except Exception:
        return [float(x) for x in init_alpha(128)]
```
